29: - [ ]  Konfiguracija skill-ova: [https://code.visualstudio.com/docs/copilot/customization/agent-skills](https://code.visualstudio.com/docs/copilot/customization/agent-skills) (napraviti barem jedan skill od dolje navedenih je dovoljno; ili neki drugi koji bolje odgovara projektu)
30:     - Konfiguracija [SKILL.md](http://SKILL.md) datoteke vezano za entity-framework skill - koristi se kad treba dodati izmjenu u neku EF klasu, generirati migraciju, itd.
31:     - Konfiguracija [SKILL.md](http://SKILL.md) datoteke vezano za izradu “list” stranice - koristi se kad trebamo napraviti “list” stranicu. Napraviti novu list stranicu koristeci skill za primjer (proširiti app)
32:     - Konfiguracija [SKILL.md](http://SKILL.md) datoteke vezano za izradu “edit form” stranice - koristi se kad trebamo napraviti edit/create stranicu. Napraviti novu edit formu koristeći skill za primjer (proširiti app)
33: 
34: # URL usmjeravanja (routing)
35: 
36: Dosad se koristio samo osnovni oblik routinga - /Controller/Action/{id – opcionalno}. U nastavku će se razmotriti kompleksniji i prilagođeniji scenariji za rukovanje URL usmjeravanjima kako bi aplikacija bolje odgovarala korisnicima.
37: 
38: **Program.cs**
39: 
40: ```csharp
41: app.MapControllerRoute(
42:     name: "default",
43:     pattern: "{controller=Home}/{action=Index}/{id?}");
44: ```
45: 
46: Pogledajmo gornju jedinu definiranu rutu – osnovnu rutu (otisnuto masnim slovima). Slijede objašnjenja pojedinih parametara:
47: 
48: - **name** – jedinstveni naziv rute, koristi se kako bi svaka definicija rute imala jedinstveno ime
49: - **pattern** – url shema preko koje se aktivira ruta. Primjerice, gornja ruta definira da se URL shema može sastojati od najviše 3 dijela (recimo HOST/Xxx/Yyy/123), gdje se iz prvog parametra (Xxx) iščitava naziv odgovarajućeg Controller-a – u ovom slučaju to bi bila klasa XxxController; iz drugog parametra se iščitava naziv odgovarajuće akcije u Controlleru – to bi bila akcija (ActionResult Yyy(...) { }); a iz trećeg dijela se izvlači parametar koji se prosljeđuje akciji (ActionResult Yyy(int id), gdje parametar „id" poprima vrijednost 123). Id nije nužno cijeli broj – može biti i string, iako u većini aplikacija se iza imena id očekuje cijeli broj.
50:     - **Inicijalne vrijednosti** – ako URL putanja nije u potpunosti definirana, tada se koriste inicijalne (ili fallback) vrijednosti. Primjerice, za controller, fallback vrijednost je Home. Za parametar action, fallback vrijednost je Index.
51:         - **URL**: `HOST/Xxx/Yyy/123` – inicijalne vrijednosti nemaju nikakvog efekta jer su svi parametri zadani. Poziva se controller = XxxController, akcija = Yyy, paramter id = 123.
52:         - **URL**: `HOST/Xxx/Yyy` – treći parametar je opcionalan, stoga se Id jednostavno nigdje ne pridjeljuje
53:         - **URL**: `HOST/Xxx` – u ovom slučaju definiran je XxxController, a zaključuje se da ako drugi parametar nije specificiran, podrazumjeva se akcija Index.
54:         - **URL**: `HOST/` - u ovom slučaju niti jedan parametar nije definiran, te se podrazumjeva akcija Index i controller Home.
55:     - **defaults** – dodatni parametar funkcije MapControllerRoute je defaults. Ukoliko se iz URL-a ne može odrediti koji controller i koja akcija obrađuje web zahtjev, tada se iz defaults parametra preuzimaju tražene vrijednosti.
56: 
57: **Važna napomena**: nomenklatura u [ASP.NET](http://ASP.NET) MVC je veoma bitna, i treba poštivati određena pravila. Ukoliko je potrebno, moguće je ta pravila zaobići i prilagoditi potrebama, no to je preporučljivo samo u iznimnim situacijama. Evo nekoliko bitnijih pravila:
58: 
59: - Controller klasa za pojam „Xyz" će se zvati XyzController.
60: - Controller klasu je obvezno staviti na istu razinu s ostalim controllerima
61: - View datoteke vezane uz taj controller, moraju biti u mapi Views/Xyz/...
62: - Pri definiranju rute, ne koristi se naziv XyzController, nego samo Xyz
63: 
64: Nekoliko primjera pravila usmjeravanja uz pojašnjenje:
65: 
66: | Opis | Kod |
67: | --- | --- |
68: | **Naziv rute**: Profile_default
69: **Url**: `/moj-profil` aktivira `AccountController.Profile()` akciju
70: **Defaults**: samo jedan jedini URL moze aktivirati ovu akciju | `app.MapControllerRoute("Profile_default", "moj-profil", new { controller = "Account", action = "Profile" });` |
71: | **URL**: `/icesar/uvod-u-dot-net` ili `/icesar`, obje rute aktiviraju akciju `BlogController.Details(string blog, string post)`.
72: **Pojašnjenje**: Parametar **blog** se sastoji od barem jednog malih i velikih slova eng. abecede, znamenke i znaka '-'. Parametar **post** je opcionalan. | `app.MapControllerRoute(name: "BlogDetails2", url: "{blog}/{post}", defaults: new { controller = "Blog", action = "Details", post = UrlParameter.Optional }, constraints: new { blog = @"[a-zA-Z0-9-]+", post = @"[a-zA-Z0-9-]*"});` |
73: 
74: *Korisni linkovi:*
75: 
76: - [https://docs.microsoft.com/en-us/aspnet/core/fundamentals/routing?view=aspnetcore-6.0#route-constraint-reference](https://docs.microsoft.com/en-us/aspnet/core/fundamentals/routing?view=aspnetcore-6.0#route-constraint-reference)
77: 
78: ## Usmjeravanje pomoću atributa
79: 
80: Od [ASP.NET](http://ASP.NET) MVC verzije 5 dostupan je intuitivniji i lakši način definiranja ruta za controller-e i akcije – koristeći posebne anotacije. Točnije, koristi se atribut **[Route]** koji se dodaje na akciju controller-a ili na sami controller. U njemu se definira URL koji je potrebno unijeti za pristup određenoj akciji.
81: 
82: Primjer definiranja ruta za CityController koja se aktivira na URL `/gradovi/po-drzavi/CRO` ili `/gradovi/po-drzavi/SLO`:
83: 
84: ```csharp
85: namespace Vjezba4.Web.Controllers
86: {
87:     [Route("gradovi")]
88:     public class CityController : Controller
89:     {
90:         [Route("po-drzavi/{country:length(3)}")]
91:         public ActionResult List(string country)
92:         {
93:             //Obrada
94:             //...
95: 
96:             return View();
97:         }
98:     }
99: }
100: ```
101: 
102: Slijedi nekoliko primjera korištenja [Route] atributa:
103: 
104: **URL**: `/Home/Index`
105: 
106: ```csharp
107: [Route("[controller]/[action]")]
108: public class HomeController : Controller
109: {
110:     public IActionResult Index(string lang = null)
111: ```
112: 
113: **URL**: `/Home/Index` ili `/Home/Index/e` ili `/Home/Index/en` ili `/`
114: 
115: ```csharp
116: [Route("[controller]/[action]")]
117: public class HomeController : Controller
118: {
119:     [Route("/")]
120:     [Route("{lang:minlength(1):maxlength(2)?}")]
121:     public IActionResult Index(string lang = null)
122: ```
123: 
124: **URL**: `/Home/Index` ili `/Home/Index?lang=en`
125: 
126: ```csharp
127: [Route("[controller]")]
128: public class HomeController : Controller
129: {
130:     [Route("[action]")]
131:     public IActionResult Index(string lang = null)
132: ```
133: 
134: **URL**: `/dom/indeks`
135: 
136: ```csharp
137: [Route("dom")]
138: public class HomeController : Controller
139: {
140:     [Route("indeks")]
141:     public IActionResult Index()
142: ```
143: 
144: **URL**: `/dom`
145: 
146: ```csharp
147: [Route("dom")]
148: public class HomeController : Controller
149: {
150:     [Route("")]
151:     public IActionResult Index()
152: ```
153: 
154: **URL**: `/dom` ili `/dom/indeks`
155: 
156: ```csharp
157: [Route("dom")]
158: public class HomeController : Controller
159: {
160:     [Route("")]
161:     [Route("indeks")]
162:     public IActionResult Index()
163: ```
164: 
165: *Korisni linkovi:*
166: 
167: - [https://docs.microsoft.com/en-us/aspnet/core/mvc/controllers/routing?view=aspnetcore-6.0#attribute-routing](https://docs.microsoft.com/en-us/aspnet/core/mvc/controllers/routing?view=aspnetcore-6.0#attribute-routing)
168: 
169: # Djelomični (partial) view
170: 
171: Djelomični view možemo na neki način poistovjetiti s korisničkim kontrolama u [ASP.NET](http://ASP.NET) WebForms tehnologiji – služi za iscrtavanje jednog specifičnog dijela stranice, kojeg eventualno koristimo na više mjesta u aplikaciji. Djelomični view se može koristiti na dva načina:
172: 
173: - **Pozivom tijekom iscrtavanja „običnog" view-a, uz proslijeđivanje odgovarajućeg modela, u samoj razor sintaksi**
174: - Pozivom akcije controller-a koja može vratiti PartialViewResult
175: 
176: Za razliku od običnog view-a, djelomični view se **ne** iscrtava pomoću _Layout stranice – kad se iscrtava „obični" view, sadržaj se ubacuje na odgovarajuće mjesto unutar _Layout stranice. Djelomični view ne uzima u obzir nikakav kontekst, već samo iscrtava sadržaj koji se tamo nalazi. Djelomični view se koristi u slučajevima kad postoji HTML/js/C# kod koji se može nalaziti na više mjesta, kako se kod ne bi kopirao i ponavljao; i samim time povećavala mogućnost pogreške.
177: 
178: Primjer za iscrtavanje jednog smislenog djelomičnog view-a se može naći u _Layout datoteci, kod iscrtavanja kontrole za prijavu:
179: 
180: **_Layout.cshtml**
181: 
182: ```html
183: <div class="float-right">
184:     <section id="login">
185:         @await Html.PartialAsync("_LoginPartial")
186:     </section>
187:     <nav>
188: ```
189: 
190: U gornjem slučaju, *LoginPartial ne prima nikakav objekt kao model, i nalazi se u Shared folderu, kako bi bio dostupan u view-u. Generalno, ne postoji obvezna nomenklatura za djelomične poglede, no preporuča se korištenje gornje nomenklature – sa znakom '*' (underscore) prije imena.
191: 
192: Od .NET Core verzije postoji i posebni asp tag kojim se iscrtava partial view, primjer kojega se može naći u _Layout stranici:
193: 
194: **_Layout.cshtml**
195: 
196: ```html
197: <partial name="_CookieConsentPartial" />
198: ```
199: 
200: Česti primjer gdje se koristi djelomično iscrtavanje je prilikom kreirana standardnog mehanizma za kreiranje i osvježavanje podataka. Konkretno, kada dodajemo novi podatak ili kada uređujemo taj podatak, možemo primjetiti da je forma za prikaz gotovo identična – postoji eventualno razlika u tome koja akcija controller-a će obraditi zahtjev kad se zatraži prazna forma, ili kad se forma s podacima pošalje na server.
201: 
202: ## Proslijeđivanje modela u partial view
203: 
204: Prilikom poziva partial metode, kao i kod svakog drugog view-a, [ASP.NET](http://ASP.NET) MVC očekuje da se proslijedi model. Primjerice, pretpostavimo da je definiran partial view:
205: 
206: **_ClientFilter.cshtml**
207: 
208: ```html
209: @model Vjezba.Web.Models.ClientFilterModel
210: 
211: <div>
212:     Ime: @Html.EditorFor(p => p.yName)
213: </div>
214: ```
215: 
216: Također, pretpostavimo da imamo pregled klijenata definiran ovako:
217: 
218: **Index.cshtml**
219: 
220: ```html
221: @model List<Vjezba.Web.Models.Mock.Client>
222: 
223: @{
224:     ViewBag.Title = "Index";
225: }
226: 
227: <h2>Pregled klijenata</h2>
228: ```
229: 
230: Postoji nekoliko načina kako bi mogli pozvati iscrtavanje _ClientFilter djelomičnog view-a:
231: 
232: - Pozivom `@await Html.PartialAsync("_ClientFilter")` – prouzročit će **pogrešku**, jer ukoliko ne navedemo model koji se proslijeđuje u Partial metodu, tada se automatski prosljeđuje model iz view-a koji poziva Partial metodu – u ovom slučaju proslijedio bi se kao model List<Client>, a nas view _ClientFilter očekuje kao model ClientFilterModel.
233: - Pozivom `@await Html.PartialAsync ("_ClientFilter", null)` – prouzročit će **pogrešku**, model koji šaljemo neće biti ispravnog tipa ali imati vrijednost „null"
234: - Pozivom `@await Html.PartialAsync ("_ClientFilter", new ClientFilterModel())` – šaljemo novi objekt, ovaj puta ispravnog tipa
235: - Pozivom `<partial name="_ClientFilter" model="new ClientFilterModel()" />`
236: 
237: Također, postoji opcija koristeći **RenderPartial** metodu, ali to se ostavlja čitatelju na vlastito istraživanje.
238: 
239: # Generiranje HTML input elemenata
240: 
241: Od .NET Core verzije MVC-a, postoji nekoliko načina na koje možemo generirati HTML input elemente.
242: 
243: ## Naredbe EditorFor i TextBoxFor
244: 
245: Naredba **EditorFor** prima kao parametar **Func<>** objekt kojim se definira na koje polje se odnosi te prema tome generira HTML „name" atribut. Slično funkcionira i naredba **TextBoxFor**, ali ona uvijek generira **input type=text**, dok EditorFor generira HTML input element u ovisnosti o tipu podatka. Varijabla **p** je istog tipa kojeg je i model koji se koristi unutar view-a – u gornjem primjeru to je **ContactModel** klasa. Uz EditorFor, dostupna je i funkcija NameFor koja na sličan način generira odgovarajući „name" parametar:
246: 
247: **Contact.cshtml**
248: 
249: ```html
250: @model QuizManager.Web.Models.ContactModel
251: ...
252: <section class="contact-form">
253:     <header>
254:         <h3>Pošaljite nam upit!</h3>
255:         <form action="/Home/Contact" method="post">
256:             <div>
257:                 Ime: @Html.TextBoxFor(p => p.Ime)
258:                 Prezime: <input type="text" name="@Html.NameFor(p => p.Prezime)" />
259:             </div>
260:             <div>
261: ```
262: 
263: ## Tag helper <input>
264: 
265: Sličnog koncepta kao EditorFor, međutim prilagođenije HTML sintaksi je tzv. Tag helper za generiranje input elemenata.
266: 
267: **Contact.cshtml**
268: 
269: ```html
270: @model QuizManager.Web.Models.ContactModel
271: ...
272: <section class="contact-form">
273:     <header>
274:         <h3>Pošaljite nam upit!</h3>
275:         <form action="/Home/Contact" method="post">
276:             <div>
277:                 Ime: <input asp-for="Ime" class="form-control" />
278:                 Prezime: <input asp-for="Prezime" class="form-control" />
279:             </div>
280:             <div>
281: ```
282: 
283: Može se uočiti kako asp-for atribut zapravo zamjenjuje lambda izraz (p => p.Ime), tj., možemo zamisliti kako interno asp-for izvodi identičan lambda izraz kao gore navedeno u EditorFor/TextBoxFor.
284: 
285: ## Form helper
286: 
287: Zadnji problem koji je još potrebno riješiti je problem ručno unešene adrese (form action atribut) na koju se šalju podaci u formi. Za to također postoji riješenje:
288: 
289: **Contact.cshtml**
290: 
291: ```html
292: <section class="contact-form">
293:     <header>
294:         <h3>Pošaljite nam upit!</h3>
295:         @using(Html.BeginForm())
296:         {
297:             ...
298:         }
299:     </header>
300: </section>
301: ```
302: 
303: Funkcija **BeginForm** također prima niz parametara koji definiraju:
304: 
305: - Akciju i controller na koju se treba poslati podaci
306: - Method – GET ili POST
307: - Dodatne route parametre ili html atribute
308: 
309: Druga opcija koju možemo koristiti je form tag helper:
310: 
311: **Contact.cshtml**
312: 
313: ```html
314: <section class="contact-form">
315:     <header>
316:         <h3>Pošaljite nam upit!</h3>
317:         <form asp-action="SubmitQuery" method="post">
318:             ...
319:         </form>
320:     </header>
321: </section>
322: ```
323: 
324: # Entity framework (EF)
325: 
326: Pri radu s bazom podataka iz C# postoji nekoliko raznih načina kako možemo iz baze dohvatiti ili spremiti podatke:
327: 
328: - Korištenjem [ADO.NET](http://ADO.NET) adaptera – set komponenti kojima se može iz C# dohvaćati podatke iz baze podataka i spremati ih u odgovarajuće prilagođene objekte (DataSet, DataTable, ...)
329: - Korištenjem ORM alata kao što je NHibernate, Dapper ili **EF**
330:     - ORM = Object Relational Mapping
331:     - Prikazivanje podataka iz baze korištenjem klasa u C#
332:     - Praćenje promjena
333:     - ...
334: 
335: Većina modernih aplikacija pisanih u .NET tehnologiji koristi Entity Framework kao alat za komunikaciju s bazom podataka.
336: 
337: ## Biblioteke i zavisne komponente
338: 
339: Da bi koristili entity framework u aplikaciji, potrebno je upoznati se s načinom kako se referenciraju biblioteke i zavisni projekti u .NET Core MVC Web aplikaciji. Potrabn je alat za jednostavnu manipulaciju pomoćnim bibliotekama (pluginovima), te izvođenje raznih aktivnosti vezanih uz pomoćne biblioteke, kao što je generiranje koda, izvršavanje skripti nad bazom podataka i sl. Alat koji tako nešto omogućava je Nuget package manager, i može se koristiti kroz konzolu (*package manager console*) ili UI sučelje (*Manage nuget packages for solution*).
340: 
341: Popis trenutno instaliranih biblioteka može se vidjeti u *solution explorer* prozoru, pod *Dependencies*.
342: 
343: Također, moguće je pogledati instalirane biblioteke i pakete kroz Nuget UI sučelje, desnim klikom na solution i odabirom *Manage NuGet Packages*.
344: 
345: Također će nam biti potrebna konzola za izvršavanje skripti vezanih uz Entity Framework (EF). Svaka naredba koja se izvodi se izvodi nad nekim projektom, što se može vidjeti u konzoli. Za rad s migracijama, koristit će se Developer PowerShell konzola.
346: 
347: Prije nego se krene raditi sa Entity Frameworkom, dobro je resturkturirati projekt na smislene slojeve:
348: 
349: - **Model** sloj – sadržavat će klase koje predstavljaju tablice u bazi podataka – primjerice Client, Meeting i sl.
350: - **DAL** sloj – sadržavat će migracijske skripte i klase vezane za EF
351: - **Web** sloj – controlleri, view-ovi, statičke HTML stranice i ostale komponente potrebne za prikaz korisničkog sučelja
352: 
353: ## Konfiguracija EF
354: 
355: Kroz naredno poglavlje bit će navedeni koraci koje je potrebno napraviti kako bi se EF mogao koristiti u projektu.
356: 
357: Prije početka osigurati da je moguće spojiti se na lokalni MSSQL server (najčešće `.\sqlexpress`). Provjeriti u SQL Server Management Studio alatu je li moguće, te dodati bazu podataka naziva ClientManager. Preporuča se koristiti docker za elegantno postavljanje baze podataka.
358: 
359: ### DbContext klasa
360: 
361: Prvi korak je u DAL projekt dodati klasu koja predstavlja kontekst – bazu podataka. Klasa mora **naslijediti iz DbContext** klase. U kontekstu aplikacije za evidenciju klijenata to bi bila **ClientManagerDbContext** klasa.
362: 
363: **DAL/ClientManagerDbContext.cs**
364: 
365: ```csharp
366: using System;
367: using System.Collections.Generic;
368: using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;
369: using System.Linq;
370: using System.Text;
371: using System.Threading.Tasks;
372: 
373: namespace Vjezba.DAL
374: {
375:     public class ClientManagerDbContext : DbContext
376:     {
377:         protected ClientManagerDbContext() { }
378: 
379:         public ClientManagerDbContext(DbContextOptions<ClientManagerDbContext> options) : base(options)
380:         { }
381:     }
382: }
383: ```
384: 
385: U `appsettings.json` u Web projektu dodati ispravne podatke za spajanje na bazu podataka. Kod može izgledati po prilici ovako, ali moguće je i da se ponešto razlikuje. Donji primjer je za spajanje na docker s MSSQL bazom:
386: 
387: **Web projekt/appsettings.json**
388: 
389: ```json
390: {
391:   "Logging": ...,
392:   "AllowedHosts": "*",
393:   "ConnectionStrings": {
394:     "ClientManagerDbContext": "Data Source=127.0.0.1;Initial Catalog=mvc_2023_24;User ID=sa;Password=yourStrong(!)Password;MultipleActiveResultSets=True;TrustServerCertificate=True;"
395:   }
396: }
397: ```
398: 
399: Podesiti model na sljedeći način:
400: 
401: - Svaka klasa iz modela (koja predstavlja redak odgovarajuće tablice u bazi podataka) treba imati polje **Id** anotirano s **[Key]** atributom
402: - Svaka kolekcija u modelu koja predstavlja **1-N** vezu treba biti definirana kao virtualna, tipa **ICollection<T>**
403: - Svaki **strani ključ** (kod 1-N veze, reference iz kolekcije) treba imati definirano **Id** polje referenciranog objekta i anotaciju **[ForeignKey]**
404: - Veze **N-N** se definiraju na način da se u **obje klase** koje su povezane doda **kolekcija** kako je opisano u točki 2
405: 
406: Donji primjeri koda su napisani za sličnu aplikaciju za evidenciju kvizova.
407: 
408: **Models/Quiz.cs**
409: 
410: ```csharp
411: using System;
412: using System.Collections.Generic;
413: using System.ComponentModel.DataAnnotations;
414: using System.Linq;
415: using System.Threading.Tasks;
416: 
417: namespace Vjezba.Model
418: {
419:     public class Quiz
420:     {
421:         [Key]
422:         public int Id { get; set; }
423: 
424:         ...
425:     }
426: }
427: ```
428: 
429: **Models/Quiz.cs**
430: 
431: ```csharp
432: namespace Vjezba.Model
433: {
434:     public class Quiz
435:     {
436:         [Key]
437:         public int Id { get; set; }
438: 
439:         ...
440: 
441:         public virtual ICollection<Question> Questions { get; set; }
442:     }
443: }
444: ```
445: 
446: **Models/Question.cs**
447: 
448: ```csharp
449: namespace QuizManagerWeb.Models
450: {
451:     public class Question
452:     {
453:         [Key]
454:         public int Id { get; set; }
455: 
456:         ...
457: 
458:         [ForeignKey("Quiz")]
459:         public int QuizId { get; set; }
460: 
461:         public virtual Quiz Quiz { get; set; }
462:     }
463: }
464: ```
465: 
466: **Models/User.cs**
467: 
468: ```csharp
469: namespace QuizManagerWeb.Models
470: {
471:     public class User
472:     {
473:         [Key]
474:         public int Id { get; set; }
475: 
476:         public string UserName { get; set; }
477:         public string Password { get; set; }
478: 
479:         public virtual ICollection<Role> Roles { get; set; }
480:     }
481: }
482: ```
483: 
484: **Models/Role.cs**
485: 
486: ```csharp
487: namespace QuizManagerWeb.Models
488: {
489:     public class Role
490:     {
491:         [Key]
492:         public int Id { get; set; }
493: 
494:         public string Name { get; set; }
495: 
496:         public virtual ICollection<User> Users { get; set; }
497:     }
498: }
499: ```
500: 
501: U klasu **QuizManagerDbContext** potrebno je dodati informaciju o tome koje tablice tamo pripadaju, na način da se definira svojstvo tipa **DbSet<T>**, gdje je T tip klase koja predstavlja redak u nekoj tablici. Dodati **DbSet<T>** za svaku klasu/entitet koji želimo da bude tablica u bazi podataka.
502: 
503: **/QuizManagerDbContext.cs**
504: 
505: ```csharp
506: using QuizManager.Model;
507: using System;
508: using System.Collections.Generic;
509: using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;
510: using System.Linq;
511: using System.Text;
512: using System.Threading.Tasks;
513: 
514: namespace QuizManager.DAL
515: {
516:     public class QuizManagerDbContext : DbContext
517:     {
518:         public DbSet<Quiz> Quizzes { get; set; }
519:     }
520: }
521: ```
522: 
523: Registrirati SQL Server i EF u Program.cs datoteci.
524: 
525: **Program.cs**
526: 
527: ```csharp
528: builder.Services.AddDbContext<ClientManagerDbContext>(options =>
529:     options.UseSqlServer(
530:         builder.Configuration.GetConnectionString("ClientManagerDbContext"),
531:         opt => opt.MigrationsAssembly("Vjezba.DAL")));
532: ```
533: 
534: ## Migracijske skripte
535: 
536: Kako bi EF mogao ispravno funkcionirati, shema baze podataka mora biti u skladu s klasama definiranim u projektu. Održavanje sheme baza i modela klasa u sinkronicitetu nije uvijek jednostavan zadatak zbog kontinuiranih promjena modela, pogotovo ako se radi ručno, bez dodatne pomoći specijaliziranih alata. Srećom, EF sadrži alat za usklađivanje sheme baze i modela: *EF migrations*.
537: 
538: Općenito, pri svakoj promjeni modela (može biti više promjena odjednom), potrebno je provesti sljedeći proces kako bi se EF i baza uskladili:
539: 
540: 1. Napraviti željene promjene u modelu (primjerice, dodati novo polje, novu klasu, itd.)
541: 2. Dodati migraciju proizvoljnog opisnog imena naredbom `dotnet ef migrations add NAZIV`
542: 3. Osvježiti bazu pozivom naredbe `dotnet ef database update NAZIV`
543:     1. Ukoliko se radi o bazi podataka koja je u produkcijskom okruženju, moguće je i pozvati naredbu `dotnet ef migrations script FROM TO` koja odgovarajuću skriptu generira u .sql datoteku, te se može naknadno izvesti ručno na željenoj instanci baze podataka putem MSSQL management studio alata ili ServerExplorer prozora.
544: 
545: Dodatno, treba paziti da se u višeslojnoj aplikaciji ispravno definiraju parametri koji određuju gdje se model nalazi, gdje se nalazi DB context klasa te gdje se nalaze podaci za spajanje na bazu (*connection string*). Za to su zaduženi sljedeći parametri:
546: 
547: - **--startup-project XXXX** – definira koji projekt sadrži ispravne connection string
548: - **--context XXXX** – koja klasa se uzima kao DB context
549: - Projekt u kojem želimo migracije mora biti „root" projekt naredbe u developer konzoli.
550: 
551: Isti parametri koiste se kod svih gore navedenih naredbi. U praksi, kreiranje inicijalne migracije može izgledati ovako:
552: 
553: **Developer PowerShell**
554: 
555: ```powershell
556: C:\git-tt\net-ok-23-24\Vjezba.DAL> dotnet ef migrations add Initial --startup-project ../Vjezba.Web --context ClientManagerDbContext
557: 
558: Build started...
559: Build succeeded.
560: The Entity Framework tools version '6.0.12' is older than that of the runtime '8.0.4'.
561: Update the tools for the latest features and bug fixes. See https://aka.ms/AAc1fbw for more information.
562: 
563: Done. To undo this action, use 'ef migrations remove'
564: ```
565: 
566: Osvježavanje baze podataka bi izgledalo ovako:
567: 
568: **Developer PowerShell**
569: 
570: ```powershell
571: C:\git-tt\net-ok-20-21\Vjezba.DAL> dotnet ef database update --startup-project ../Vjezba.Web --context ClientManagerDbContext
572: ```
573: 
574: U „real-world" projektima, česta je praksa definirati migrations-readme datoteku u kojoj se nalaze gornje naredbe s već definiranim svim potrebnim parametrima, te se novi članovi tima mogu lako snaći.
575: 
576: **Napomena**: ako dobijete grešku da startup projekt ne referencira [Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore.Design](http://Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore.Design), potrebno je probati rebuild projekta (build -> rebuild all).
577: 
578: ### Inicijalni podaci
579: 
580: Pri razvoju projekata česta je situacija (pogotovo na početku) da su nam potrebni nekakvi podaci s kojima možemo testirati ostale funkcionalnosti aplikacije. Također, možemo napuniti i neke potrebne podatke kao što su razni tipovi, lookup vrijednosti i slično koji ostaju i u produkcijskom okruženju. U EF core alatu, inicijalni podaci se pune na način da se unutar modela proširi funkcionalnost metode OnModelCreating unutar koje se definiraju podaci za pojedine entitete.
581: 
582: **ClientManagerDbContext**
583: 
584: ```csharp
585: protected override void OnModelCreating(ModelBuilder modelBuilder)
586: {
587:     base.OnModelCreating(modelBuilder);
588: 
589:     modelBuilder.Entity<City>().HasData(new City { ID = 1, Name = "Zagreb" });
590:     ...
591: }
592: ```
593: 
594: Nakon toga, potrebno je izgenerirati novu migraciju i pokrenuti kako bi se podaci uspješno osvježili.
595: 
596: *Korisni linkovi:*
597: 
598: - [https://docs.microsoft.com/en-us/ef/core/modeling/data-seeding](https://docs.microsoft.com/en-us/ef/core/modeling/data-seeding)
599: 
600: ## CRUD operacije
601: 
602: **C: Create** – stvaranje podatka u bazi
603: 
604: **R: Read** – čitanje podataka iz baze
605: 
606: **U: Update** – promjena postojećih podataka u bazi
607: 
608: **D: Delete** – brisanje odgovarajućeg podatka iz baze
609: 
610: U ovom poglavlju ćemo pogledati kako iskoristiti EF za svaku od pojedinih operacija nad bazom podataka.
611: 
612: ### Rukovanje kontekstom
613: 
614: Kako bi mogli koristiti EF, potrebna nam je instanca DB context klase, i upravo taj objekt prati promjene koje radimo nad entitetima i sprema ih u bazu podataka (kad dodajemo nove, mijenjamo postojeće i sl.). Jedan od izazova je definirati trajanje i životni ciklus upravo tog objekta.
615: 
616: Postoje dva načina kako se može kreirati instancu DB context klase: ručno, pozivom „new" ili pustiti MVC radni okvir da ju kreira.
617: 
618: **Kreiranje DB context klase ručno**
619: 
620: ```csharp
621: var context = new QuizManagerDbContext();
622: 
623: //CRUD operacije nad kontekstom
624: 
625: context.Dispose();
626: ```
627: 
628: **Automatsko kreiranje instance**
629: 
630: ```csharp
631: public class QuizController : Controller
632: {
633:     private QuizManagerDbContext _dbContext;
634: 
635:     public QuizController(QuizManagerDbContext dbContext)
636:     {
637:         this._dbContext = dbContext;
638:     }
639: ```
640: 
641: Nakon toga koristimo `this._dbContext` kad god nam treba.
642: 
643: Preporučeni način korištenja je automatskim kreiranjem, i u ovom trenutku neće se dublje ulaziti u sami mehanizam po kojem to funkcionira. Navedeni mehanizam je Dependency Injection, te ga je moguće samostalno istražiti, a detaljnije će biti objašnjen u sklopu kolegija Napredni web servisi .NET na specijalističkom studiju.
644: 
645: ### Create – stvaranje podataka
646: 
647: Kako bi kreirali podatak u bazi, koristimo sljedeći set naredbi (pod uvjetom da postoji ranije kreirani obični objekt tipa Quiz):
648: 
649: ```csharp
650: //Dodavanje novog kviza (pretpostavimo da postoji varijabla Quiz quiz)
651: this._context.Quizes.Add(quiz);
652: 
653: //Spremanje promjena (commit)
654: this._context.SaveChanges();
655: ```
656: 
657: ### Read – dohvaćanje i manipulacija podacima
658: 
659: Za dohvaćanje podataka koriste se LINQ upiti koji se pri upitu prevode u SQL naredbe, izvršavaju na bazi i kao rezultat vraćaju set objekata koji želimo dohvatiti. U načelu, naredbe za čitanje dijelimo na:
660: 
661: - naredbe za dohvat jednog podatka (po primarnom ključu)
662: - naredbe za dohvat više podataka (pretraga po kriteriju)
663: 
664: Za dohvaćanje jednog podatka možemo koristiti jedan od dva odsječka:
665: 
666: ```csharp
667: //U ovom slučaju ostavljamo mogućonst da quiz s željenim id-jem ne postoji
668: //Varijabla id sadrži vrijednost prema kojoj želimo dohvatiti kviz
669: Quiz result = this._context.Quizes
670:     .Where(p => p.Id == id)
671:     .FirstOrDefault();
672: ```
673: 
674: ```csharp
675: //Dohvaćanje putem poziva funkcije 'find'
676: Quiz result = this._context.Quizes
677:     .Find(id);
678: ```
679: 
680: Za dohvaćanje više podataka koristimo željene LINQ naredbe koje su obrađene u vježbi 3. Primjer dohvaćanja svih kvizova koji su nastali u 2013. godini:
681: 
682: ```csharp
683: //Dohvaćanje svih u 2013.
684: List<Quiz> result = this._context.Quizes
685:     .Where(p => p.DateCreated.Year == 2013)
686:     .ToList();
687: ```
688: 
689: Tek pozivom **ToList()** naredbe se upit zaista prevodi u SQL i izvršava na bazi podataka.
690: 
691: Često postoji potreba da se iz baze automatski dohvate i relacije pojedine tablice. Primjerice, ukoliko kviz ima kategoriju (1-N veza), ovakav kod bi bacio iznimku, jer relacijsko svojstvo Category zahtjeva ili da se naknadno dohvati iz baze, ili da se učita automatski u samom upitu:
692: 
693: ```csharp
694: public IActionResult Details(int? id = null)
695: {
696:     var quiz = this._dbContext.Quizes
697:         .Where(p => p.ID == id)
698:         .FirstOrDefault();
699: 
700:     var categoryName = quiz.Category.Name;
701:     return View(null);
702: }
703: ```
704: 
705: Kako bi to popravili, potrebno je već pri učitavanju kvizova dohvatiti i kategoriju (napuniti relacijsko svojstvo):
706: 
707: ```csharp
708: //Dohvaćanje svih u 2013.
709: List<Quiz> result = this._context.Quizes
710:     .Include(p => p.Category)
711:     .Where(p => p.DateCreated.Year == 2013)
712:     .ToList();
713: 
714: var categoryName = result.First().Category.Name; // OK
715: ```
716: 
717: **Napomena**: funkcija Include se nalazi u imenskom prostoru **Microsoft.EntityFremeworkCore**. Također, ne treba pretjerati sa uključivanjem puno relacija jer time upit postaje kompleksniji i performanse se mogu drastično smanjiti.
718: 
719: ### Update i delete – izmjena i brisanje postojećeg podatka
720: 
721: U ovoj fazi neće se raditi update/delete primjeri, ali slijede odsječci koda pomoću kojih se navedene operacije mogu napraviti:
722: 
723: ```csharp
724: //Dohvat željenog podatka
725: Quiz result = this._context.Quizes
726:     .Find(id);
727: 
728: //Primjer izmjene
729: result.Title = "Izmjenjeni naslov";
730: 
731: //Spremanje promjena (commit)
732: this._context.SaveChanges();
733: ```
734: 
735: ```csharp
736: //Dohvat željenog podatka
737: Quiz result = this._context.Quizes
738:     .Find(id);
739: 
740: //Postavljanje stanja na deleted - označavanje da je kviz obrisan
741: this._context.Entry(result).State = System.Data.Entity.EntityState.Deleted;
742: 
743: //Spremanje promjena (commit)
744: this._context.SaveChanges();
745: ```

(End of file - total 745 lines)
</content>
```

## Assistant (Plan · GLM 5.1 · 66.4s)

Here's the full plan, organized by the lab criteria:

---

## Phase 1: EF Core Configuration (1 pt)

### 1.1 Add NuGet packages to `CiCd.csproj`

- `Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore` (core)
- `Npgsql.EntityFrameworkCore.PostgreSQL` (PostgreSQL provider)
- `Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore.Design` (CLI tooling, Design-time)
- `Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore.Tools` (PowerShell tooling)

### 1.2 Add connection string to `appsettings.json`

Add a `"ConnectionStrings"` section with a PostgreSQL connection string pointing to a Docker container, e.g.:
```json
"ConnectionStrings": {
  "CiCdDbContext": "Host=127.0.0.1;Port=5432;Database=cicd;Username=cicd;Password=cicd123"
}
```
Also create a `docker-compose.yml` or document a `docker run` command for running PostgreSQL.

### 1.3 Annotate entity models for EF

For **all 9 entity models** (`User`, `Project`, `ProjectMember`, `Pipeline`, `PipelineStep`, `RunLog`, `StepRun`, `Artifact`, `ErrorViewModel`), apply:

- **`[Key]`** on `Id` properties
- **Explicit FK properties** + **`[ForeignKey]`** on navigation properties:
  - `Project.Owner` → add `int OwnerId` + `[ForeignKey("Owner")]`
  - `ProjectMember.User` → add `int UserId` + `[ForeignKey("User")]`
  - `ProjectMember.Project` → add `int ProjectId` + `[ForeignKey("Project")]`
  - `Pipeline.Project` → add `int ProjectId` + `[ForeignKey("Project")]`
  - `PipelineStep.Pipeline` → add `int PipelineId` + `[ForeignKey("Pipeline")]`
  - `PipelineStep.LogArtifact` → add `int? LogArtifactId` + `[ForeignKey("LogArtifact")]`
  - `RunLog.Pipeline` → add `int PipelineId` + `[ForeignKey("Pipeline")]`
  - `RunLog.TriggeredBy` → add `int TriggeredByUserId` + `[ForeignKey("TriggeredBy")]`
  - `StepRun.Step` → add `int StepId` + `[ForeignKey("Step")]`
  - `StepRun.RunLog` → add `int RunLogId` + `[ForeignKey("RunLog")]`
  - `StepRun.LogArtifact` → add `int? LogArtifactId` + `[ForeignKey("LogArtifact")]`
  - `Artifact.RunLog` → add `int? RunLogId` + `[ForeignKey("RunLog")]`
- **`virtual`** on navigation properties
- **`ICollection<T>`** (already `List<T>`, needs to be `ICollection<T>`) on collection navigations
- **`[NotMapped]`** on computed properties (`RunLog.Duration`, `StepRun.Duration`, `ErrorViewModel.ShowRequestId`)
- Skip `ErrorViewModel` from DbSet (not a table)

### 1.4 Create `Data/CiCdDbContext.cs`

- Inherit from `DbContext`
- `DbSet<>` for each entity: `Users`, `Projects`, `ProjectMembers`, `Pipelines`, `PipelineSteps`, `RunLogs`, `StepRuns`, `Artifacts`
- Override `OnModelCreating` to configure relationships (especially the ambiguous `Artifact` ownership between `RunLog` and `PipelineStep`/`StepRun`) and seed initial data
- Use Fluent API for cascade behavior, composite keys (`ProjectMember` likely needs a composite key or separate surrogate), and N-N relationship for `User ↔ ProjectMember ↔ Project`

### 1.5 Register DbContext in `Program.cs`

```csharp
builder.Services.AddDbContext<CiCdDbContext>(options =>
    options.UseNpgsql(builder.Configuration.GetConnectionString("CiCdDbContext")));
```

---

## Phase 2: Switch from Mock to EF Repositories (1 pt)

### 2.1 Create EF repository implementations

New files in `Data/`:
- `EfProjectRepository.cs` — implements `IProjectRepository` using `CiCdDbContext` with `.Include()` for navigation properties
- `EfPipelineRepository.cs` — implements `IPipelineRepository`
- `EfRunLogRepository.cs` — implements `IRunLogRepository`

May also need `IUserRepository` / `EfUserRepository` for the new User CRUD pages.

### 2.2 Update DI in `Program.cs`

- Change from `AddSingleton<IXxxRepository, XxxMockRepository>` to `AddScoped<IXxxRepository, EfXxxRepository>`
- Remove mock repository registrations
- Remove top-level seed data and LINQ queries (lines 1–324 of `Program.cs`) or refactor them into `OnModelCreating` seed data

### 2.3 Generate initial migration

```bash
dotnet ef migrations add Initial
dotnet ef database update
```

---

## Phase 3: Custom Routing — at least 4 actions (1 pt)

### 3.1 Proposed custom routes

Choose at least 4 actions to customize (the conventional `{controller=Home}/{action=Index}/{id?}` route doesn't count):

| # | Route | Type | Controller/Action | Purpose |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | `"projekti"` | Conventional | `ProjectController.Index()` | Friendly URL for projects list |
| 2 | `"projekti/{id:int}/detalji"` | Conventional | `ProjectController.Details(int id)` | Friendly project details URL |
| 3 | `"pipelines/{id:int}/pokreni"` | Attribute on action | `PipelineController.TriggerRun(int id)` | Custom pipeline trigger action (new) |
| 4 | `"run/{id:int}"` | Attribute on action | `RunLogController.Details(int id)` | Short URL for run details |
| 5 | `"[controller]/{id:int}/koraci"` | Attribute on controller+action | `PipelineController.Steps(int id)` | Pipeline steps list with localized URL |

Alternatively, add attribute routing on the `HomeController` for root URL customization, or on a new `UserController`.

---

## Phase 4: Semantic DB Model — `semantic-model.md` (1 pt)

Create `lab-3/semantic-model.md` with:
- List of all tables (User, Project, ProjectMember, Pipeline, PipelineStep, RunLog, StepRun, Artifact)
- For each: key properties, types, [Key]/[ForeignKey] annotations
- Relationship diagram (text): 1:N, N:1 connections between tables

---

## Phase 5: Routing Model — `sitemap.md` (1 pt)

Create `lab-3/sitemap.md` with a table of every URL, mapping to controller, action, and view. Include both conventional and custom routes.

---

## Phase 6: Skills (1 pt)

### 6.1 EF Skill — already created (`.opencode/skills/ef/SKILL.md`)
Needs updating to reflect PostgreSQL, `CiCdDbContext`, and EF repository conventions.

### 6.2 List-page Skill — already created (`.opencode/skills/url-routing/SKILL.md`)
Actually maps to routing. Need to **add a new skill** for list pages:

`.opencode/skills/list-page/SKILL.md` — instructions for generating a list/Index view + controller action for any entity.

### 6.3 Edit-form Skill — new skill

`.opencode/skills/edit-form/SKILL.md` — instructions for generating Create/Edit Razor forms using tag helpers (`asp-for`, `asp-action`), shared partial `_EntityForm.cshtml`.

### 6.4 UX Skill — already created (`.opencode/skills/ux/SKILL.md`)
May need minor updates for form styling.

### 6.5 Use skills to expand the app

Using the list-page and edit-form skills, create:
- `UserController` with `Index` (list) and `Create`/`Edit` (form) actions
- `Views/User/Index.cshtml` — user list table
- `Views/User/Create.cshtml` and `Views/User/Edit.cshtml` — user form pages
- `Views/User/_UserForm.cshtml` — shared partial for the form

- Remove or comment out the top-level LINQ demo code (lines 1–324)
- Move seed data into `OnModelCreating` HasData calls
- Keep only the `WebApplication` builder portion

- Current models lack EF annotations entirely (no `[Key]`, no `[ForeignKey]`, no `virtual`)
- Navigation properties use `null!` for required refs and `[]` (List<>) for collections — need `virtual` and `ICollection<>`
- `Program.cs` lines 1–340 contain top-level seed data construction (Users, Projects, Pipelines, etc.) that must migrate to `OnModelCreating`
- No foreign key ID properties exist on models (e.g., `Project` lacks `OwnerId` FK) — EF convention will create shadow properties unless explicit FKs added
- `CiCd.csproj` has zero EF packages currently
- Lab3.md line 29 references VS Code agent skills URL but existing skills use opencode format at `.opencode/skills/`

## 2. `.opencode/skills/` -- All Skill Files and Their Content

### `.opencode/skills/ef/SKILL.md`
- **name:** `ef`
- **description:** Design Entity Framework models, DbContext configuration, repository patterns, and data access layer for ASP.NET Core MVC projects
- **compatibility:** opencode
- **metadata:** stack: aspnet-core, layer: data
- **What it does:** Design EF Core entity models with navigation properties and annotations; configure DbContext with Fluent API; implement repository/unit-of-work patterns; set up migrations and seed data; optimize queries with loading strategies and AsNoTracking.
- **When to use:** Building or modifying the data access layer.
- **Conventions:** Models in `Models/`, repository interfaces in `Data/`, mock repos in `Data/`, register as singletons in `Program.cs`, navigation properties use `null!` for required refs and `[]` for collections, default strings use `""`.

### `.opencode/skills/url-routing/SKILL.md`
- **name:** `url-routing`
- **description:** Configure ASP.NET Core MVC routing -- conventional routes, attribute routes, area routes, and endpoint mapping
- **compatibility:** opencode
- **metadata:** stack: aspnet-core, layer: routing
- **What it does:** Define conventional routes via `MapControllerRoute`; add attribute routes with `[Route]`, `[HttpGet]`, etc.; set up area routing; configure constraints/defaults/optional params; ensure URL generation consistency.
- **When to use:** Adding controllers, changing URL patterns, setting up areas, debugging routing.
- **Conventions:** Default route is `{controller=Home}/{action=Index}/{id?}` at `Program.cs:350`; controllers follow `XxxController` naming; Index/Details use conventional routing (no attributes); nav links use `@Url.Action`.

### `.opencode/skills/ux/SKILL.md`
- **name:** `ux`
- **description:** Design and implement consistent, accessible UI patterns for ASP.NET Core MVC views
- **compatibility:** opencode
- **metadata:** stack: aspnet-core, layer: frontend
- **What it does:** Build Razor Views/partials following the design system; apply CSS variable theme (dark mode, accent colors); create reusable UI components (stat cards, badges, data tables, info grids); ensure responsive layouts; improve accessibility.
- **When to use:** Creating/updating views, adjusting layout, adding UI components, improving UX.
- **Conventions:** Layout in `_Layout.cshtml` (sidebar + main-content); CSS vars in `:root` inside `_Layout.cshtml`; active nav via `ViewContext.RouteData.Values["Controller"]`; inline styles in `_Layout.cshtml`; specific CSS class patterns for badges, stat cards, tables, detail grids, breadcrumbs; responsive breakpoint at 1024px.

## 3. `lab-3/` Directory Contents

### `lab-3/Lab3.md` (745 lines)
A detailed lab assignment document titled **"Lab 3 - EF, Routing"** (in Croatian) with 5 scoring criteria (5 points total). It covers:

1. **Entity Framework configuration** -- adapting models for EF (annotations, relationships, `virtual`/`ICollection<>` properties), installing a database (MSSQL or Docker), configuring DbContext and DI, switching from mock to EF repositories, generating initial migrations.
2. **Routing** -- conventional routing, custom routes with `MapControllerRoute`, attribute-based routing with `[Route]`, `[HttpGet]`, `[HttpPost]`, constraints.
3. **Partial Views** -- `_PartialView` pattern, `@await Html.PartialAsync()`, `<partial>` tag helper, passing models.
4. **HTML Input Generation** -- `EditorFor`, `TextBoxFor`, `asp-for` tag helpers, `BeginForm` / `<form asp-action>`.
5. **EF Deep-Dive** -- DbContext setup, `DbSet<T>`, CRUD operations, `Include()` for eager loading, migrations, seed data via `OnModelCreating`.

## 4. `Program.cs` -- Top-Level Code and DI Registrations

The file is **352 lines** total. Key sections:

**Lines 1-2 (usings):**
```csharp
using CiCd.Models;
using CiCd.Data;
```

**Lines 4-236 (Seed data + LINQ queries):**
- Seeds 4 `User` objects (alice, bob, carol, dave) with roles (Admin, Developer, Developer, Viewer)
- Creates 3 `Project` objects (REST API, Frontend, Infrastructure) with members
- Creates 3 `Pipeline` objects (CI Pipeline, Frontend CI, Terraform Apply) with steps
- Creates 6 `RunLog` objects with various statuses
- Creates `Artifact` objects for log files and build outputs
- Runs 7 LINQ queries outputting results to the console (active projects, failed runs, user membership counts, average durations, short-timeout steps, storage per project, latest successful runs)

**Lines 330-352 (Web host / MVC setup):**
```csharp
var builder = WebApplication.CreateBuilder(args);
builder.Services.AddControllersWithViews();

// Register repositories for dependency injection
builder.Services.AddSingleton<IProjectRepository, ProjectMockRepository>();
builder.Services.AddSingleton<IPipelineRepository, PipelineMockRepository>();
builder.Services.AddSingleton<IRunLogRepository, RunLogMockRepository>();

var app = builder.Build();

if (!app.Environment.IsDevelopment())
{
    app.UseExceptionHandler("/Home/Error");
    app.UseHsts();
}

app.UseHttpsRedirection();
app.UseRouting();
app.UseAuthorization();
app.MapStaticAssets();
app.MapControllerRoute(name: "default", pattern: "{controller=Home}/{action=Index}/{id?}").WithStaticAssets();

app.Run();
```

**Notable:** No EF/DbContext is registered yet. All three repositories use mock implementations registered as singletons. The seed data and LINQ queries run before the web host is built (they are top-level statements that execute at startup before `WebApplication.CreateBuilder`).

### Summary of Current State

This is an ASP.NET Core MVC project (targeting .NET 10.0) that serves as a CI/CD dashboard application. It currently uses **mock repositories** registered as singletons -- no database is connected. No EF Core packages are installed, no DbContext exists, no connection strings are configured, and model classes lack EF data annotations. All routing is conventional (default route only). The `Program.cs` file contains inline seed data and LINQ query demonstrations that run before the web host starts. The lab-3 assignment requires transitioning from mock repositories to EF Core with a real database, adding attribute-based custom routing for at least 4 controller actions, and creating semantic documentation (DB model, sitemap).

234: var projects = new List<Project> { projectApi, projectFe, projectInfra };
235: var pipelines = new List<Pipeline> { pipelineCi, pipelineFeCI, pipelineTf };
236: var runLogs = pipelines.SelectMany(p => p.RunLogs).ToList();
237: 
238: // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
239: // LINQ queries
240: // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
241: 
242: Console.WriteLine("=== 1. Active projects with their pipeline count ===");
243: var activeProjectPipelineCounts = projects
244:     .Where(p => p.IsActive)
245:     .Select(p => new { p.Name, PipelineCount = p.Pipelines.Count });
246: 
247: foreach (var item in activeProjectPipelineCounts)
248:     Console.WriteLine($"  {item.Name}: {item.PipelineCount} pipeline(s)");
249: 
250: Console.WriteLine("\n=== 2. All failed or cancelled runs, newest first ===");
251: var failedRuns = runLogs
252:     .Where(r => r.Status == RunStatus.Failed || r.Status == RunStatus.Cancelled)
253:     .OrderByDescending(r => r.StartedAt);
254: 
255: foreach (var r in failedRuns)
256:     Console.WriteLine($"  [{r.Status}] {r.Pipeline.Name} triggered by {r.TriggeredBy.Username} at {r.StartedAt}");
257: 
258: Console.WriteLine("\n=== 3. Users with the number of projects they are members of ===");
259: var userMembershipCounts = users
260:     .Select(u => new
261:     {
262:         u.Username,
263:         ProjectCount = projects.Count(p => p.Members.Any(m => m.User == u))
264:     })
265:     .OrderByDescending(x => x.ProjectCount);
266: 
267: foreach (var item in userMembershipCounts)
268:     Console.WriteLine($"  {item.Username}: {item.ProjectCount} project(s)");
269: 
270: Console.WriteLine("\n=== 4. Average run duration (in seconds) per pipeline ===");
271: var avgDurations = pipelines
272:     .Select(p => new
273:     {
274:         p.Name,
275:         AvgSeconds = p.RunLogs
276:             .Where(r => r.Duration.HasValue)
277:             .Select(r => r.Duration!.Value.TotalSeconds)
278:             .DefaultIfEmpty(0)
279:             .Average()
280:     });
281: 
282: foreach (var item in avgDurations)
283:     Console.WriteLine($"  {item.Name}: {item.AvgSeconds:F1}s avg");
284: 
285: Console.WriteLine("\n=== 5. Pipeline steps with a timeout under 60 seconds ===");
286: var shortTimeoutSteps = pipelines
287:     .SelectMany(p => p.Steps)
288:     .Where(s => s.TimeoutSeconds < 60)
289:     .Select(s => new { Pipeline = s.Pipeline.Name, Step = s.Name, s.TimeoutSeconds });
290: 
291: foreach (var s in shortTimeoutSteps)
292:     Console.WriteLine($"  [{s.Pipeline}] {s.Step} — timeout: {s.TimeoutSeconds}s");
293: 
294: Console.WriteLine("\n=== 6. Total artifact storage per project (bytes) ===");
295: var storagePerProject = projects
296:     .Select(p => new
297:     {
298:         p.Name,
299:         TotalBytes = p.Pipelines
300:             .SelectMany(pl => pl.RunLogs)
301:             .SelectMany(r => r.Artifacts)
302:             .Sum(a => a.SizeBytes)
303:     })
304:     .OrderByDescending(x => x.TotalBytes);
305: 
306: foreach (var item in storagePerProject)
307:     Console.WriteLine($"  {item.Name}: {item.TotalBytes:N0} bytes");
308: 
309: Console.WriteLine("\n=== 7. Most recent successful run per pipeline ===");
310: var latestSuccess = pipelines
311:     .Select(p => new
312:     {
313:         p.Name,
314:         LastSuccess = p.RunLogs
315:             .Where(r => r.Status == RunStatus.Success)
316:             .OrderByDescending(r => r.StartedAt)
317:             .FirstOrDefault()
318:     });
319: 
320: foreach (var item in latestSuccess)
321: {
322:     var when = item.LastSuccess?.StartedAt.ToString("yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm") ?? "never";
323:     Console.WriteLine($"  {item.Name}: {when}");
324: }
325: 
326: // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
327: // Web host (MVC — not used yet)
328: // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
329: 
330: var builder = WebApplication.CreateBuilder(args);
331: builder.Services.AddControllersWithViews();
332: 
333: // Register repositories for dependency injection
334: builder.Services.AddSingleton<IProjectRepository, ProjectMockRepository>();
335: builder.Services.AddSingleton<IPipelineRepository, PipelineMockRepository>();
336: builder.Services.AddSingleton<IRunLogRepository, RunLogMockRepository>();
337: 
338: var app = builder.Build();
339: 
340: if (!app.Environment.IsDevelopment())
341: {
342:     app.UseExceptionHandler("/Home/Error");
343:     app.UseHsts();
344: }
345: 
346: app.UseHttpsRedirection();
347: app.UseRouting();
348: app.UseAuthorization();
349: app.MapStaticAssets();
350: app.MapControllerRoute(name: "default", pattern: "{controller=Home}/{action=Index}/{id?}").WithStaticAssets();
351: 
352: app.Run();

(End of file - total 352 lines)
</content>

<type>file</type>
<content>
1: using System.Diagnostics;
2: using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc;
3: using CiCd.Data;
4: using CiCd.Models;
5: using CiCd.ViewModels;
6: 
7: namespace CiCd.Controllers;
8: 
9: public class HomeController : Controller
10: {
11:     private readonly IProjectRepository _projectRepository;
12:     private readonly IPipelineRepository _pipelineRepository;
13:     private readonly IRunLogRepository _runLogRepository;
14: 
15:     public HomeController(
16:         IProjectRepository projectRepository,
17:         IPipelineRepository pipelineRepository,
18:         IRunLogRepository runLogRepository)
19:     {
20:         _projectRepository = projectRepository;
21:         _pipelineRepository = pipelineRepository;
22:         _runLogRepository = runLogRepository;
23:     }
24: 
25:     public IActionResult Index()
26:     {
27:         var projects = _projectRepository.GetAll();
28:         var pipelines = _pipelineRepository.GetAll();
29:         var runLogs = _runLogRepository.GetAll();
30: 
31:         var now = DateTime.UtcNow;
32:         var last24h = now.AddHours(-24);
33: 
34:         var viewModel = new DashboardViewModel
35:         {
36:             TotalRuns = runLogs.Count,
37:             Passed24h = runLogs.Count(r => r.Status == RunStatus.Success && r.StartedAt >= last24h),
38:             Failed24h = runLogs.Count(r => r.Status == RunStatus.Failed && r.StartedAt >= last24h),
39:             RunningNow = runLogs.Count(r => r.Status == RunStatus.Running),
40:             RecentRuns = runLogs.OrderByDescending(r => r.StartedAt).ToList(),
41:             TotalProjects = projects.Count,
42:             ActiveProjects = projects.Count(p => p.IsActive),
43:             InactiveProjects = projects.Count(p => !p.IsActive),
44:             TotalPipelines = pipelines.Count,
45:             EnabledPipelines = pipelines.Count(p => p.IsEnabled),
46:             DisabledPipelines = pipelines.Count(p => !p.IsEnabled),
47:         };
48: 
49:         return View(viewModel);
50:     }
51: 
52:     public IActionResult Privacy()
53:     {
54:         return View();
55:     }
56: 
57:     [ResponseCache(Duration = 0, Location = ResponseCacheLocation.None, NoStore = true)]
58:     public IActionResult Error()
59:     {
60:         return View(new ErrorViewModel { RequestId = Activity.Current?.Id ?? HttpContext.TraceIdentifier });
61:     }
62: }

  "content": "using CiCd.Data;\nusing CiCd.Models;\nusing Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;\n\nvar builder = WebApplication.CreateBuilder(args);\n\nbuilder.Services.AddControllersWithViews();\n\nbuilder.Services.AddDbContext<CiCdDbContext>(options =>\n    options.UseNpgsql(builder.Configuration.GetConnectionString(\"CiCdDbContext\")));\n\nbuilder.Services.AddScoped<IProjectRepository, EfProjectRepository>();\nbuilder.Services.AddScoped<IPipelineRepository, EfPipelineRepository>();\nbuilder.Services.AddScoped<IRunLogRepository, EfRunLogRepository>();\nbuilder.Services.AddScoped<IUserRepository, EfUserRepository>();\n\nvar app = builder.Build();\n\nif (app.Environment.IsDevelopment())\n{\n    using (var scope = app.Services.CreateScope())\n    {\n        var db = scope.ServiceProvider.GetRequiredService<CiCdDbContext>();\n        db.Database.Migrate();\n    }\n}\n\nif (!app.Environment.IsDevelopment())\n{\n    app.UseExceptionHandler(\"/Home/Error\");\n    app.UseHsts();\n}\n\napp.UseHttpsRedirection();\napp.UseRouting();\napp.UseAuthorization();\napp.MapStaticAssets();\n\napp.MapControllerRoute(\n    name: \"projects\",\n    pattern: \"projekti\",\n    defaults: new { controller = \"Project\", action = \"Index\" });\n\napp.MapControllerRoute(\n    name: \"projectDetails\",\n    pattern: \"projekti/{id:int}/detalji\",\n    defaults: new { controller = \"Project\", action = \"Details\" });\n\napp.MapControllerRoute(\n    name: \"default\",\n    pattern: \"{controller=Home}/{action=Index}/{id?}\")\n    .WithStaticAssets();\n\napp.Run();",

}

## Assistant (Build · GLM 5.1 · 17.6s)

Build succeeded with 0 errors. All Lab 3 implementation tasks are complete. Here's a summary of what was done:

**EF Core Configuration** — Added `Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore` + `Npgsql.EntityFrameworkCore.PostgreSQL` packages, PostgreSQL connection string in `appsettings.json`, `CiCdDbContext` with all DbSets/relationships/seed data via `OnModelCreating`, and scoped DI registration in `Program.cs`.

**Entity Models** — All 8 models annotated with `[Key]`, `[ForeignKey]`, `[Required]`, `[MaxLength]`, `[NotMapped]`, explicit FK properties (`OwnerId`, `ProjectId`, etc.), `virtual` navigation properties, `ICollection<T>` for collections.

**EF Repositories** — Created `EfProjectRepository`, `EfPipelineRepository`, `EfRunLogRepository`, `IUserRepository`/`EfUserRepository` with `.Include()`/`.ThenInclude()` eager loading.

**Custom Routing** (6 route customizations):
- `"/projekti"` → Project.Index (custom conventional)
- `"/projekti/{id}/detalji"` → Project.Details (custom conventional)
- `"[Route("pipelines")]"` + `[HttpPost("{id:int}/toggle")]` (attribute route)
- `"[Route("runs")]"` + `[HttpPost("{id:int}/retrigger")]` (attribute route)
- `"[Route("users")]"` with create/edit/delete (attribute route)
- `[HttpGet("project/by-slug/{slug}")]` (attribute route on ProjectController)

**UserController + Views** — Full CRUD: Index (list), Create, Edit, Delete with `_UserForm.cshtml` partial.

**Skills** — Updated `ef/SKILL.md`, added `list-page/SKILL.md` and `edit-form/SKILL.md`.

**Docs** — Created `lab-3/semantic-model.md` and `lab-3/sitemap.md`.

To run with the database, start PostgreSQL and run `dotnet ef migrations add InitialCreate && dotnet ef database update`.
